const KURANG={11:25000,23:25000};
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const rp=n=>'Rp'+Math.round(n).toLocaleString('id-ID');
let cur=null,ri=0,ten=35;
const pot=(m,t)=>Math.max(0,m.p-(KURANG[t]||0));
const ang=(m,r,t)=>r[1+m.t.indexOf(t)]*1000-pot(m,t);
function grid(){
  const q=$('q').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  $('grid').innerHTML=D.m.map((m,i)=>({m,i})).filter(x=>x.m.n.toLowerCase().includes(q)).map(x=>
    `<button class="card${x.m===cur?' on':''}" data-i="${x.i}"><strong>${x.m.n}</strong><small class="mono">OTR ${rp(x.m.o)}</small></button>`).join('')||'<p style="padding:12px">Tipe tidak ditemukan</p>';
}
function pick(i){
  cur=D.m[i];ri=0;
  if(!cur.t.includes(ten))ten=cur.t.includes(35)?35:cur.t[cur.t.length-1];
  grid();draw();
}
function draw(){
  const m=cur,n=m.r.length,r=m.r[ri],dp=r[0]*1000;
  $('dp').textContent=rp(dp);
  $('pc').textContent=(dp/m.o*100).toFixed(1).replace('.',',')+'% dari OTR';
  const rg=$('rg');rg.max=n-1;rg.value=ri;
  $('lo').textContent=rp(m.r[0][0]*1000);$('hi').textContent=rp(m.r[n-1][0]*1000);
  $('ten').innerHTML=m.t.map(t=>`<button class="${t===ten?'on':''}" data-t="${t}">${t} bln</button>`).join('');
  $('tt').textContent='ANGSURAN '+m.n.toUpperCase()+' (RP)';
  const s=Math.max(0,Math.min(ri-3,n-7)),rows=m.r.slice(s,s+7);
  $('tb').innerHTML='<tr><th>DP</th>'+m.t.map(t=>`<th>${t} bln</th>`).join('')+'</tr>'+
    rows.map((x,k)=>`<tr class="${s+k===ri?'sel':''}" data-r="${s+k}"><td>${x[0]*1000/1e6}jt</td>`+m.t.map(t=>`<td class="mono${t===ten?' c':''}">${ang(m,x,t).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</td>`).join('')+'</tr>').join('');
  const i=1+m.t.indexOf(ten),nor=r[i]*1000,pt=pot(m,ten),a=nor-pt,kg=KURANG[ten]||0,rin=kg?`${rp(m.p)} - ${rp(kg)}`:rp(m.p);
  $('rc').innerHTML=`<div class="t mono"><span>ESTIMASI ANGSURAN</span><span>${ten} BLN</span></div><h3>${m.n}</h3>
  <div class="big mono">${rp(a)} <i>/bulan</i></div>
  <dl class="mono"><div><dt>Harga OTR</dt><dd>${rp(m.o)}</dd></div><div><dt>Uang muka</dt><dd>${rp(dp)}</dd></div><div><dt>Tenor</dt><dd>${ten} bulan</dd></div><div><dt>Angsuran PL reguler</dt><dd>${rp(nor)}</dd></div>${pt?`<div><dt>Potongan angsuran</dt><dd>-${rp(pt)}</dd></div><div><dt>(${rin})</dt><dd></dd></div>`:''}<div><dt>Total bayar</dt><dd>${rp(dp+a*ten)}</dd></div></dl>`;
  updWA(m,dp,nor,pt,rin,a);
}
function updWA(m,dp,nor,pt,rin,a){
  const L=['*SIMULASI KREDIT HONDA*','*SPS Motor - 29 Tahun*','',`Tipe: *${m.n}*`,`Harga OTR: ${rp(m.o)}`,`Uang muka (DP): ${rp(dp)}`,`Tenor: ${ten} bulan`,'',`Angsuran PL reguler: ${rp(nor)}`];
  if(pt)L.push(`Potongan angsuran: -${rp(pt)}`);
  L.push(`*Angsuran: ${rp(a)} / bulan*`);if(pt)L.push(`*Hemat: ${rp(pt*ten)}*`);L.push('','Estimasi, dapat berubah sesuai ketentuan leasing.','','✅ Terima tukar tambah','✅ Gratis cek motor lamamu','✅ Gratis cuci motor setahun, di setiap bulan');
  let n=$('no').value.replace(/\D/g,'');if(n[0]==='0')n='62'+n.slice(1);else if(n[0]==='8')n='62'+n;
  $('wa').href='https://wa.me/'+n+'?text='+encodeURIComponent(L.join('\n'));
}
const SUB=[["Revo Fit",18160000,1500000,1400000,100000,800000],["Revo X",19880000,1600000,1400000,200000,800000],["BeAT 8%",20360000,1700000,1400000,300000,800000,1],["BeAT",20360000,2100000,1600000,500000,800000],["Genio",21300000,2200000,1600000,600000,800000],["Supra X 125 Spoke",21350000,1800000,1400000,400000,800000],["Supra X 125 CW",22390000,1800000,1400000,400000,800000],["Scoopy",23940000,2400000,1600000,800000,800000],["Vario 125",25420000,2600000,1800000,800000,750000],["Vario Street 8%",27680000,2800000,1800000,1000000,1000000,1],["Vario 160",29010000,3000000,2000000,1000000,1000000],["Stylo",30020000,2500000,1200000,1300000,300000],["PCX",34960000,3500000,1900000,1600000,800000],["ADV",36900000,3700000,1900000,1800000,800000]];
const nf=n=>n.toLocaleString('id-ID');
$('ts').innerHTML='<tr><th>Tipe</th><th>OTR</th><th>DP PO</th><th>Subs</th><th>Bayar</th><th>Cash</th></tr>'+SUB.map(r=>`<tr class="${r[6]?'hl':''}"><td>${r[0]}</td><td class="mono">${nf(r[1])}</td><td class="mono">${nf(r[2])}</td><td class="mono">${nf(r[3])}</td><td class="mono bayar">${nf(r[4])}</td><td class="mono">${nf(r[5])}</td></tr>`).join('');
$('q').oninput=grid;
$('grid').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('.card');if(b)pick(+b.dataset.i)};
$('ten').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('button');if(b){ten=+b.dataset.t;draw()}};
$('tb').onclick=e=>{const r=e.target.closest('tr[data-r]');if(r){ri=+r.dataset.r;draw()}};
$('rg').oninput=e=>{ri=+e.target.value;draw()};
$('mi').onclick=()=>{ri=Math.max(0,ri-1);draw()};
$('pl').onclick=()=>{ri=Math.min(cur.r.length-1,ri+1);draw()};
$('no').oninput=draw;pick(0);
</script><script>if('serviceWorker' in navigator)navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js');</script></body></html>
